# Gig: Desperate Measures

Journal: `quests/street_stories/santo_domingo/arroyo/sts_std_arr_12` · `StreetStory` · hash `3778538461`.

Journal premise: Gig type: Thievery\nObjective: Steal an automated Zetatech vehicle\nLocation: Motel on Wollesen St.\nDetails:\n\nBetter get some tissues ready – this one's gonna be a real tearjerker. Pedro Aimar, a guy who thinks he's some kinda nova netrunner cuz he coded a few algorithms for Zetatech. Well, now he wants to klep their ass. It's not what you think – see, he has some kinda health problems and Zetatech doesn't wanna chip in for his operation. Crazy, right? A corp that doesn't give a shit about its employee. I'm practically speechless. Well, now look at that... tears are splashing onto my screen. This is a life and death situation, V! Pedro needs our help! He (and we) reeeaally need the eddies!\n\nPedro wants to klep a Zetatech truck full of A-grade tech (already got a buyer). Meet up with Pedro and figure out how you wanna play this. If you help him, our readiness to help a poor soul in need will be handsomely rewarded.

**Coverage: journal-linked.** 84 exact journal-linked nodes in 3 resources; 8 resources in the inspected dependency context.

Resource roles in that context: 7 content candidate, 1 repair. Role labels describe path/name evidence, not proof of runtime reachability.

## Required acceptance cases before reuse

Exercise normal completion and each named alternate outcome; interrupt and return at every scene handoff; start with spatial/item/actor predicates already satisfied; save/load across waits and joins; cancel parallel listeners; verify reward delivery once; and verify cleanup without changing the committed outcome. Apply the block-specific cases in the [expanded contracts](../all-quest-blocks.md). No installed playtest is claimed.

Shared forward dependencies can serve several quests; these are context counts.
